## Sync workflow

1. Check DESIGN_SYNC.md → [PENDING CHANGES] table for what needs implementing
2. For each row: open the .dc.html → translate to .tsx → wire API → push
3. Mark the row `✅ [commit SHA]` in DESIGN_SYNC.md when done
4. **There is no token export step and there never was.** This line read "run
   `node scripts/export-tokens.js > src/app/design-tokens.css` if DS tokens
   changed" until 2026-09-15; **neither file has ever existed in any commit on
   any branch** (`git log --all` returns zero for both), so every session that
   went to re-export tokens found nothing and moved on. Tokens are
   **hand-maintained in `src/app/globals.css`** — `:root` plus the six
   `[data-theme]` blocks — and a value change is measured rather than
   generated: `npm run audit:contrast` re-derives every ink against its own
   ground, and `npm run audit:retro` refuses a raw colour literal on a
   member-facing route. The design system's own token files live under
   `design/handoff-console-2026-08-21/tokens/` and are a READING, not an
   input; the row at the top of this file explains why its `colors.css` must
   not be adopted.

## API client

**There is no `lib/api.js`.** This section read "`lib/api.js` — use this as the
API route reference. All endpoints are listed there" until 2026-09-15, and that
file has never existed in any commit on any branch — an instruction pointing at
a route index nobody could open, in a repository with ~180 API routes. The route
tree **is** the reference: `src/app/api/`, one directory per path, and
`npm run audit:published-urls` resolves every URL the product publishes against
it. Two guards stand behind it, and they are the reason a hand-written index
would be worse than none: `src/lib/__tests__/api-method-callers.test.ts` fails
when an exported HTTP method has no caller in `src/`, `scripts/`, `e2e/`,
`workers/` or `public/` (its first run retired five handlers nothing called),
and `audit:mounts` asks the same question of components. A list in a file cannot
go red; both of these do.

## Navigation

**There are TWO navs and neither is copied from anywhere.** This section
described one bar — "Listen|Events|Pages", implemented by copying
`lib/NavShell.js` + `lib/shell.css` from Claude Design — until 2026-09-15.
Those two files have never existed in any commit on any branch, and the bar they
described was retired with the legacy shell: `SiteNavTabs` and
`MobileBottomNav` are DELETED and `guard:design` asserts they stay deleted.

- **ONE navigation, since 2026-09-22 (DESIGN_SYNC row 500):** the site header,
  `src/components/AdaptiveSiteHeader.tsx`, on every page a member reaches —
  the mark on the left and the four destinations as text links on the right,
  **Listen · Map · Tickets · Me**, declared once in `src/lib/mmm-nav.ts`. Inside
  `/app/*` it is slim (`--app-header-h`) and the shell's frame starts at its
  bottom edge; the shell's only chrome is the now-playing pill
  (`src/components/mmm/MmmNowPlaying.tsx`), present while a track is loaded.
  `npm run measure:chrome` drives both. The walnut dock
  (`src/components/mmm/MmmDock.tsx`) and the public pages' bottom tab bar
  (`src/components/SiteTabBar.tsx`) are DELETED and `guard:design` asserts they
  stay deleted; the two bullets this replaces described them as live.
- **Where the header is drawn** is `src/lib/chrome-visibility.ts`'s decision: a
  page that owns the whole screen (the admin console) gets no member chrome,
  read from `x-pathname` in the ROOT LAYOUT rather than from a marker in markup
  — a CSS rule keyed on a descendant cannot hide chrome that renders before the
  descendant.

## DO / DO NOT

| DO | DO NOT |
|---|---|
| **Read the existing .tsx before touching it** | **Overwrite a file without reading it first** |
| Use Edit tool for UI updates | Use Write tool to replace whole files |
| Translate .dc.html faithfully to .tsx | Invent UI not shown in .dc.html |
| Use `var(--*)` CSS tokens from design system | Hardcode colors or type sizes |
| Wire real API to existing mock data shapes | Change data structure without design update |
| Add `admin@ihype.org` for all contact | Use any other email address |
| Keep split as 70/20/10 / 0% iHYPE | Change the revenue split in any copy |
| Use audio-only for radio/live | Add video hosting or live video |
| Reference `ihype.org` only | Use any other domain |
| Preserve auth gates and DB queries on UI updates | Remove or replace backend logic during UI syncs |
